local Tool = script.Parent;
local Ball = Tool.Handle
local ms = game.Players.LocalPlayer:GetMouse()

enabled = true
function onButton1Down(mouse)
	if not enabled then
		return
	end

	enabled = false
	mouse.Icon = "rbxasset://textures\\GunWaitCursor.png"

	wait(.2)
	mouse.Icon = "rbxasset://textures\\GunCursor.png"
	enabled = true

end

function onEquippedLocal(mouse)


	if mouse == nil then
		print("Mouse not found")
		return 
	end
	Tool.Enabled = true

	mouse.Icon = "rbxasset://textures\\GunCursor.png"
	mouse.Button1Down:connect(function() onButton1Down(mouse) end)
end

Tool.Enabled = true
Tool.Activated:connect(function()
	if ms == nil or not Tool.Enabled then
		return
	end
	local character = Tool.Parent;
	local humanoid = character.Humanoid
	if humanoid == nil then
		print("Humanoid not found")
		return 
	end
	local targetPos = ms.Hit.p
	local lookAt = (targetPos - character.Head.Position).unit
	Tool.ThrowBall:FireServer(lookAt)
	wait(2)
end)


Tool.Equipped:connect(onEquippedLocal)

--Made by Luckymaxer

Mouse_Icon = "rbxasset://textures/GunCursor.png"
Reloading_Icon = "rbxasset://textures/GunWaitCursor.png"

Tool = script.Parent

Mouse = nil

function UpdateIcon()
	if Mouse then
		Mouse.Icon = Tool.Enabled and Mouse_Icon or Reloading_Icon
	end
end

function OnEquipped(ToolMouse)
	Mouse = ToolMouse
	UpdateIcon()
end

function OnChanged(Property)
	if Property == "Enabled" then
		UpdateIcon()
	end
end

Tool.Equipped:connect(OnEquipped)
Tool.Changed:connect(OnChanged)f
=== chunk=419 type=PROP offset=61686 runSha256=5f53978e9fe1ca4228abbab288c4f95c510730af4fe276ca55b027aa06b59bb6 ===
--// wonuf -- Probably really messy, but it works..so :D

script.Name = "core"

local Player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local Mouse = Player:GetMouse()

--// Settings

local SplashSound = 142431247

local rFunction = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.fishUpdate

if not game.Workspace:findFirstChild("FishRodBin") then
	Bin = Instance.new("Backpack", game.Workspace)
	Bin.Name = "FishRodBin"
else
	Bin = game.Workspace.FishRodBin
end

local Tool = script.Parent
local WireStart = Tool:WaitForChild("Tip")

--// Initialisation

local Part, Wire, Catching, Enabled = nil, nil, false, false

--// Functions

function DrawFromPoints(a, b)
	if Wire then
		Wire:Destroy()
	end
	Wire = Instance.new('Part',Bin)
	Wire.CanCollide = false
	Wire.Anchored = true
	Wire.BrickColor = BrickColor.new('Light stone grey')
	Wire.FormFactor = Enum.FormFactor.Custom
	Wire.Size = Vector3.new(.4, 0, .2)
	
	local Mesh = Instance.new('CylinderMesh', Wire)
	Mesh.Scale = Vector3.new(1, 1, 0.3)
	
	Wire.Size = Vector3.new(1,(a - b).magnitude, 0.3)
	Wire.CFrame = CFrame.new(a, b) * CFrame.new(0, 0, -(a - b).magnitude / 2) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90),0,0)
	
	game.Debris:AddItem(Wire, 60)
end

function throw()
	if Mouse.Hit and Mouse.Target and Mouse.Target.Name == "Water" then
		DrawFromPoints(WireStart.Position, Mouse.Hit.p)
		local P = Instance.new("Part")
		P.Name = Player.Name.."Part"
		P.Anchored = true
		P.Shape = "Ball"
		P.Size = Vector3.new(1,1,1)
		P.BrickColor = BrickColor.Red()
		P.CanCollide = false
		P.Transparency = 0
		P.TopSurface = "Smooth"
		P.BottomSurface = "Smooth"
		P.CFrame = CFrame.new(Mouse.Hit.p + Vector3.new(0,.2,0))
		P.Parent = Bin
		Part = P
		game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(Part,60)
	end
end

function bob()
	if Enabled then
		Catching = true
		local s = Instance.new("Sparkles", Part)
		game.Debris:AddItem(s, 1)
		if not Player.PlayerGui:findFirstChild("BobSound") then
			local x = Instance.new("Sound", Player.PlayerGui)
			x.Name = "BobSound"
			x.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..SplashSound
			x:play()
			game.Debris:AddItem(x, 2)
		end
		delay(1, function() if Catching and Enabled and Part and Wire then 
			Enabled, Catching = false, false Part:Destroy() Wire:Destroy() wait(1)
			end 
		end)
	end
end

function vowelCheck(fish)
	local vowels = {"a","e","i","o","u"}
	for i,v in pairs(vowels) do
		if string.sub(string.lower(fish),1,1) == v then
			return true
		end
	end
end

function catch()
	local fish,price = rFunction:InvokeServer(Player.userId)
	local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui",game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui)
	game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(gui,3)
	local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	label.Position = UDim2.new(.2,0,1,0)
	label.Size = UDim2.new(.6,0,0,50)
	if vowelCheck(fish) then
		label.Text = "You caught an "..fish.."! (+$"..price..")"
	else
		label.Text = "You caught a "..fish.."! (+$"..price..")"
	end
	label.TextColor3 = Color3.new(0,214/255,1)
	label.TextStrokeTransparency = .3
	label.Font = "SourceSansBold"
	label.FontSize = "Size48"
	label.Parent = gui
	label:TweenPosition(UDim2.new(.2,0,.25,0),"Out", "Quad", .3)
	wait(2.4)
	label:TweenPosition(UDim2.new(.2,0,0,-60),"In", "Quad", .3)
end

--// Main

Tool.Equipped:connect(function(Mouser)
	Mouser.Button1Down:connect(function()
		if not Catching then
			if not Enabled and not Catching then
				if Mouse.Hit and Mouse.Target and Mouse.Target.Name == "Water" then
					Enabled = true
					catchtimer = 0
					timetocatch = math.random(5,15)
					throw()
				end
			elseif Enabled then
				Part:Destroy()
				Wire:Destroy()
				Enabled = false
			end
		else
			if Enabled and Catching then
				Part:Destroy()
				Wire:Destroy()
				Catching, Enabled = false, false
				local catchco = coroutine.create(catch)
				coroutine.resume(catchco)
			end
		end
	end)
end)

Tool.Unequipped:connect(function()
	if Part then
		Part:Destroy()
	end
	if Wire then
		Wire:Destroy()
	end
	Catching, Enabled = false, false
end)

game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:connect(function()
	if Enabled and Part and Wire then
		local a, b = WireStart.Position, Part.Position
		Wire.Size = Vector3.new(1,(a - b).magnitude, 0.3)
		Wire.CFrame = CFrame.new(a, b) * CFrame.new(0, 0, -(a - b).magnitude / 2) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), 0, 0)
		if (Player.Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart").Position - Part.Position).Magnitude > 30 then
			Part:Destroy()
			Wire:Destroy()
			Catching, Enabled = false, false
		end
	end
end)

function changeTimer()
	while true do
		wait(1)
		if Enabled then
			catchtimer = catchtimer + 1
			if catchtimer == timetocatch then
				bob()
			end
		end
	end
end

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.RequestToLoadGui.Event:connect(function(guiname,newname,itemcategory,objectreference)
	local activeguis = 0
	for i,v in pairs(plrgui:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name:match("ACTIVE_") then
			activeguis = activeguis + 1
		end
	end
	if activeguis == 0 then
		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.LoadLocalGui:Fire(guiname,newname,itemcategory,objectreference)
	else
		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.LocalNotification:Fire("Please close the currently active window before opening a new one.")
	end
end)

end)

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.WaitToLoadGui.Event:connect(function(guiname,newname,itemcategory,objectreference) --wait for currently active GUI to disappear, then load the queued GUI
	
	local expectedgui = {guiname,newname}
	queuedgui = expectedgui --if these two end up differing, just stop the loop
	
	local function checkForActiveGuis()
		for i,v in pairs(plrgui:GetChildren()) do
			if v.Name:match("ACTIVE_") then
				return true
			end
		end
		return false
	end
	
	repeat wait(1/30) until checkForActiveGuis() == false or expectedgui ~= queuedgui
	
	if expectedgui ~= queuedgui then return end
	
	game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.RequestToLoadGui:Fire(queuedgui[1],queuedgui[2],itemcategory,objectreference)
	
end)

function FireSmokeGUICheck(nextsong)
	local activegui = plrgui:FindFirstChild("FireSmokeGUI")
	if activegui then
		activegui:Destroy()
	end

	if nextsong and nextsong.PlayerName.Value == plr.Name then
		--it's this player's turn to have the GUI
		local newgui = plrgui.LoadableGuis:FindFirstChild("FireSmokeGUI"):clone()
		newgui.Parent = plrgui
		newgui.Frame.Visible = true
	end

end

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NextSongInQueue.OnClientEvent:connect(function(nextsong)
	if game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.LocalGamePassCheck:Invoke("UnrestrictedDJ") == true then
		FireSmokeGUICheck(nextsong)
	end
end)

local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local gui = plr:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
local mouse = plr:GetMouse()
local container = gui:WaitForChild("ContextActionsGui")

if plr.Character == nil or plr.Character.Parent == nil then
	plr.CharacterAdded:wait()
end

local char = plr.Character
local hrp = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local playerstatus = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:WaitForChild(plr.userId):WaitForChild("SpecialStatus")

local CurrentObject = nil
local actions = {}

local hover_icon = "rbxassetid://231195429"
local normal_icon = "rbxasset://textures/ArrowFarCursor.png"
local select_sound = "rbxassetid://231188062"

--[[

on RenderStepped: 

IF NO TOOL EQUIPPED:


IF NO CONTEXT MENU OPEN:
- get mouse Hit. if the object's ContextActionsModelName is the same as the CurrentObjectHoveringOver, change nothing.
- if ContextActionsModelName is different (or there is currently a CurrentObjectHoveringOver, and a new ContextActionsModelName is not found), then remove the "Interact with" overlay if there is one and set mouse icon to White. if there is a ContextActionsModelName in the new object, make that the overlay and set mouse icon to Green.

- every frame of this, update any "Interact with" overlay's position


IF CONTEXT MENU OPEN:
- if mouse is outside of context menu's range, remove context menu




otherwise, IF TOOL EQUIPPED: 
- remove any overlays, do not change mouse icon







on MouseButton1Down:
IF NO CONTEXT MENU OPEN:
- if mouse is over object, generate the object's action menu. set mouse icon to White

IF CONTEXT MENU OPEN:
- if mouse is not over context menu, remove context menu

each action button has its own Clicked connection which will run the action's module and then remove the context menu

]]

function isOffScreen(point,bx,by) --bx and by are part of AbsoluteSize
	if point.x < 0 or point.x > bx or point.y < 0 or point.y > by then
		return true
	else
		return false
	end
end

function checkOffScreenCorners(element) --corners are like quadrants
	local pos = element.Position
	local size = element.Size
	local c1 = Vector2.new(pos.X.Offset + size.X.Offset, pos.Y.Offset)
	local c2 = Vector2.new(pos.X.Offset, pos.Y.Offset)
	local c3 = Vector2.new(pos.X.Offset, pos.Y.Offset + size.Y.Offset)
	local c4 = Vector2.new(pos.X.Offset + size.X.Offset, pos.Y.Offset + size.Y.Offset)
	local bx = container.AbsoluteSize.x
	local by = container.AbsoluteSize.y
	return isOffScreen(c1,bx,by),isOffScreen(c2,bx,by), isOffScreen(c3,bx,by),isOffScreen(c4,bx,by)
end

function handsAreFree()
	if plr.Character then
		for i,v in pairs(plr.Character:GetChildren()) do
			if v:IsA("Tool") or v:IsA("HopperBin") then
				return false
			end
		end
		for i,v in pairs(gui:GetChildren()) do
			if v.Name:match("ACTIVE_") then
				return false
			end
		end
		return true
	end
end

function getAvailableActions(obj)
	local getactions = {}
	local objname = obj:FindFirstChild("ContextActionsModelName")
	if objname then
		objname = objname.Value
		local objdata = game.ReplicatedStorage.ContextActions:FindFirstChild(objname)
		if objdata then
			
			for i,v in pairs(objdata:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("StringValue") then
					table.insert(getactions,v)
				end
			end
			
			local owner = obj:FindFirstChild("Owner")
			if owner then
				if owner.Value == plr.Name then
					for i,v in pairs(objdata.Owner:GetChildren()) do
						if v:IsA("StringValue") then
							table.insert(getactions,v)
						end
					end
					for i,v in pairs(objdata.Owner.SpecialStatus:GetChildren()) do
						if playerstatus:FindFirstChild(v.Name) then --if this status name is inside the player's special statuses
							for i,x in pairs(v:GetChildren()) do --add any actions that are under that category
								table.insert(getactions,x)
							end
						end
					end
				else
					if owner.Value == "" then
						for i,v in pairs(objdata.NoOwner:GetChildren()) do
							if v:IsA("StringValue") then
								table.insert(getactions,v)
							end
						end
					else
						for i,v in pairs(objdata.OtherOwner:GetChildren()) do
							if v:IsA("StringValue") then
								table.insert(getactions,v)
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			for i,v in pairs(objdata.SpecialStatus:GetChildren()) do
				if playerstatus:FindFirstChild(v.Name) then 
					for i,x in pairs(v:GetChildren()) do
						table.insert(getactions,x)
					end
				end
			end
			
			for i,v in pairs(objdata.NotSpecialStatus:GetChildren()) do
				if not playerstatus:FindFirstChild(v.Name) then 
					for i,x in pairs(v:GetChildren()) do
						table.insert(getactions,x)
					end
				end
			end
			
			
		end
	end
	actions = getactions
end

gui:WaitForChild("ActionMenuSelection").Event:connect(function(num)
	if CurrentObject ~= nil then
		local objname = CurrentObject:FindFirstChild("ContextActionsModelName")
		if objname then
			objname = objname.Value
			local objdata = game.ReplicatedStorage.ContextActions:FindFirstChild(objname)
			if objdata then
				local action = objdata:FindFirstChild(num,true)
				if action then
					local module = require(action.ModuleScript)
					module.RunAction(plr,CurrentObject) --run the action now!
				end
			end
		end
	end
end)

local RMBdown = false

function WithinValidDistance(hit,target)
	local model = target.Parent
	local maxdistance = 32
	if model:FindFirstChild("ContextActionsDistance") then
		maxdistance = model.ContextActionsDistance.Value
	end
	if char and hrp and (hrp.Position - hit.p).magnitude < maxdistance then
		return true
	else
		return false
	end
end

function MouseOverActiveFrame(x,y)
	local isover = false
	local function recurse(instance)
		for _,v in pairs(instance:GetChildren()) do
			if (v:IsA("Frame") or v:IsA("ScrollingFrame")) and v.Visible == true then
				if (x > v.AbsolutePosition.x and x < v.AbsolutePosition.x + v.AbsoluteSize.x and y > v.AbsolutePosition.y and y < v.AbsolutePosition.y + v.AbsoluteSize.y) then
					isover = true
					break
				else
					recurse(v)
				end
			end
		end
	end
	if gui:FindFirstChild("MainMenu") then
		recurse(gui.MainMenu)
	end
	if not isover then
		if gui:FindFirstChild("PhoneGUI") and gui.PhoneGUI.Frame.Visible == true then
			recurse(gui.PhoneGUI)
		end
	else
		return true
	end
	if not isover then
		if gui:FindFirstChild("HouseEditMode") and gui.HouseEditMode.CenterFrame.Visible == true then
			recurse(gui.HouseEditMode.CenterFrame)
		end
	else
		return true
	end
	if not isover then
		return false
	else
		return true
	end
end

game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:connect(function()
	local actionmenu = container:FindFirstChild("ActionMenu")
	local overlay = container:FindFirstChild("InteractOverlay")
	if handsAreFree() then
		if not actionmenu then --manage interact overlay if there's no action menu
			local targetmodel
			if mouse.Target then
				targetmodel = (mouse.Target.Parent:FindFirstChild("ContextActionsModelName") or mouse.Target.Parent:IsA("Accoutrement") and mouse.Target.Parent.Parent:FindFirstChild("ContextActionsModelName"))
			end
			if targetmodel then
				targetmodel = targetmodel.Parent
			end
			if mouse.Target and targetmodel and not RMBdown and WithinValidDistance(mouse.Hit,mouse.Target) and not MouseOverActiveFrame(mouse.X,mouse.Y) then
				if targetmodel ~= CurrentObject then --if object is valid and it is not the same one being hovered over already, get actions
					getAvailableActions(targetmodel)
					if #actions > 0 then
						CurrentObject = targetmodel
						if overlay then
							overlay:Destroy()
						end
						mouse.Icon = hover_icon
						overlay = container.BaseInteractOverlay:clone()
						overlay.Visible = true
						overlay.Name = "InteractOverlay"
						if #actions == 1 then
							overlay.Text = actions[1].Value
						else
							local modelname = targetmodel.ContextActionsModelName.Value
							if modelname == "Player" then
								modelname = targetmodel.Name
							end
							overlay.Text = "Interact with " ..modelname
						end
						overlay.Parent = container
						overlay.Visible = true
						overlay.Size = UDim2.new(0,overlay.TextBounds.X + 15,0,16)
					end
				end
			else --remove overlay if not hovering over valid object - either no CAMN, too far away, hovering over active GUI frame, or RMB is down
				mouse.Icon = normal_icon
				CurrentObject = nil
				if overlay then
					overlay:Destroy()
				end
			end
			if overlay then
				overlay.Position = UDim2.new(0,mouse.X,0,mouse.Y - overlay.Size.Y.Offset)
				local c1,c2,c3,c4 = checkOffScreenCorners(overlay)
				if c4 == true and c1 == true then
					overlay.Position = overlay.Position - UDim2.new(0,overlay.Size.X.Offset,0,0)
				end
				if c1 == true and c2 == true then
					overlay.Position = overlay.Position + UDim2.new(0,0,0,overlay.Size.Y.Offset)
				end
			end
		else
			local x = mouse.X
			local y = mouse.Y
			if x < actionmenu.Position.X.Offset - 40 or x > actionmenu.Position.X.Offset + actionmenu.Size.X.Offset + 40 or y < actionmenu.Position.Y.Offset - 40 or y > actionmenu.Position.Y.Offset + actionmenu.Size.Y.Offset + 40 then
				mouse.Icon = normal_icon
				actionmenu:Destroy()
				CurrentObject = nil
			end
		end
	else
		if actionmenu then
			actionmenu:Destroy()
		end
		if overlay then
			overlay:Destroy()
		end
		CurrentObject = nil
	end
end)

mouse.Button1Down:connect(function()
	if handsAreFree() then
		local actionmenu = container:FindFirstChild("ActionMenu")
		if not actionmenu then 
			
			if CurrentObject ~= nil and #actions > 0 then --create an action menu for the object, then set mouse icon
				local sound = Instance.new("Sound",gui)
				sound.SoundId = select_sound
				sound:Play()
				sound.TimePosition = .05
				game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound,.5)
				if #actions >= 2 then
					mouse.Icon = normal_icon
					local overlay = container:FindFirstChild("InteractOverlay")
					if overlay then
						overlay:Destroy()
					end
					actionmenu = container.BaseActionMenu:clone()
					actionmenu.Name = "ActionMenu"
					actionmenu.Parent = container
					actionmenu.Visible = true
					local maxwidth = 0
					for i,v in pairs(actions) do
						local button = actionmenu.BaseSelector:clone()
						button.Name = v.Name
						button.Text = " "..v.Value
						button.Parent = actionmenu
						button.Visible = true
						button.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0, (#actionmenu:GetChildren() - 2) * 20)
						if button.TextBounds.X > maxwidth then
							maxwidth = button.TextBounds.X
						end
						
						if plr:FindFirstChild("GamepadMode") and i == 1 then
							game:GetService("GuiService").SelectedObject = button
						end
					end
					actionmenu.Size = UDim2.new(0,maxwidth + 3,0,(#actionmenu:GetChildren() - 1) * 20)
					actionmenu.Position = UDim2.new(0,mouse.X,0,mouse.Y)
					local c1,c2,c3,c4 = checkOffScreenCorners(actionmenu)
					if c1 == true and c3 == true and c4 == true then
						actionmenu.Position = actionmenu.Position - UDim2.new(0,actionmenu.Size.X.Offset,0,actionmenu.Size.Y.Offset)
					elseif c3 == true and c4 == true then
						actionmenu.Position = actionmenu.Position - UDim2.new(0,0,0,actionmenu.Size.Y.Offset)
					elseif c1 == true and c4 == true then
						actionmenu.Position = actionmenu.Position - UDim2.new(0,actionmenu.Size.X.Offset,0,0)
					end
				else
					gui.ActionMenuSelection:Fire(actions[1].Name) --only one action
				end
			end
			
		else --action menu already open - is mouse in range of it?
			local x = mouse.X
			local y = mouse.Y
			if x < actionmenu.Position.X.Offset or x > actionmenu.Position.X.Offset + actionmenu.Size.X.Offset or y < actionmenu.Position.Y.Offset or y > actionmenu.Position.Y.Offset + actionmenu.Size.Y.Offset then
				actionmenu:Destroy()
				CurrentObject = nil
			end
		end
	end
end)

mouse.Button2Down:connect(function() --remove on right click outside of menu
	RMBdown = true
	if handsAreFree() then
		local actionmenu = container:FindFirstChild("ActionMenu")
		if actionmenu then 
			local x = mouse.X
			local y = mouse.Y
			if x < actionmenu.Position.X.Offset or x > actionmenu.Position.X.Offset + actionmenu.Size.X.Offset or y < actionmenu.Position.Y.Offset or y > actionmenu.Position.Y.Offset + actionmenu.Size.Y.Offset then
				actionmenu:Destroy()
				CurrentObject = nil
			end
		end
	end
end)

mouse.Button2Up:connect(function()
	RMBdown = false
end)

if plr:FindFirstChild("GamepadMode") then
	local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
	
	UserInputService.InputBegan:connect(function(input,gameprocessed)

				end
			end
		end
	end)

local TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")
TeleportService.CustomizedTeleportUI = true

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.TeleportToUpdateServer.OnClientEvent:connect(function()
	local gui = script.Parent:WaitForChild("TeleportGui"):clone()
	gui.Parent = script.Parent
	local frame = gui:WaitForChild("Frame"):WaitForChild("FirstFrame")
	frame.Parent.Visible = true
	wait(2/30)
	for i=1,10 do
		for _,v in pairs(frame:GetChildren()) do
			v.BackgroundTransparency = v.BackgroundTransparency - .1
		end
		wait(1/30)
	end
	TeleportService:Teleport(301377387,nil,nil,gui)
end)

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.TPToSharpshooterMines.OnClientEvent:connect(function()
	if game.Players.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("GamepadMode") then
		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.LocalNotification:Fire("Unable to teleport; this game is unavailable on console.")
		return
	end
	local sound = Instance.new("Sound",script.Parent)
	sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://153613030"
	sound:Play()
	local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui",script.Parent)
	gui.Name = "Teleportation"
	local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	label.Text = ""
	label.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
	label.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
	label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	label.Parent = gui
	local steps = 0
	game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:connect(function()
		steps = steps + 1
		if steps <= 14 then
			label.BackgroundTransparency = label.BackgroundTransparency - .072
		elseif steps <= 30 then
			local num = (255 - (steps - 14)*16)/255
			label.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(num,num,num)
		end
	end)
	repeat wait() until steps > 33
	TeleportService:Teleport(177543484)
end)

local closedpos = UDim2.new(-1,2,.6,-140)
local peekingpos = UDim2.new(0,-42,.6,-140)
local openpos = UDim2.new(0,2,.6,-140)

local frame = script.Parent:WaitForChild("Frame")
local bar = script.Parent:WaitForChild("PassingTimeLeft"):WaitForChild("Bar")

local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
if plr.Character == nil or plr.Character.Parent == nil then plr.CharacterAdded:wait() end
local char = plr.Character
local torso = plr.Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")

function isInSchool(pos) --and during class hours
	local day = game.ReplicatedStorage.Day.Value
	local t = game.Lighting:GetMinutesAfterMidnight()
	if require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.PositionIsInsideArea)(pos,"School") then
		return true
	else
		return false
	end
end

function PlayBell()
	if torso and workspace:FindFirstChild("TeleportLocations") and workspace.TeleportLocations:FindFirstChild("schoolfront") then
		local vol = 0
		if isInSchool(torso.Position) then
			vol = 1
		else
			local dist = (torso.Position - workspace.TeleportLocations.schoolfront.Position).magnitude
			vol = 1 - (dist / 450)
			if dist > 350 then
				vol = 0
			end
		end
		local sound = Instance.new("Sound",script)
		sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://345830614"
		sound.Volume = vol
		sound:Play()
	end
end

function OpenWindow()
	frame.Visible = true
	frame:TweenPosition(openpos,nil,nil,1.3,true)
	PlayBell()
end

function StartClosingWindow()
	frame:TweenPosition(peekingpos,nil,nil,4,true)
	wait(4)
end

function CloseWindow()
	frame:TweenPosition(closedpos,nil,nil,1.3,true)
	wait(1.3)
	frame.Visible = false
end


frame:WaitForChild("CloseWindow").Event:connect(function()
	CloseWindow()
end)

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.ScheduledTeleport.OnClientEvent:connect(function(period,class)
	frame.Visible = true
	if class ~= "Lunch" and class ~= "Dismissal" and class ~= "Passing Time" and class ~= "School Start" then
		frame.Header.Text = class
		frame.Period.Text = period.." is starting!"
		frame.TeleportButton.Visible = true
		frame.CloseButton.Visible = true
		OpenWindow()
		wait(30)
		StartClosingWindow()
		CloseWindow()
	elseif class == "Lunch" then
		frame.Header.Text = class
		frame.Period.Text = period.." is starting!"
		frame.TeleportButton.Visible = false
		frame.CloseButton.Visible = false
		OpenWindow()
		wait(6)
		CloseWindow()
	elseif class == "Dismissal" then
		frame.Header.Text = class
		frame.Period.Text = "School is over!"
		frame.TeleportButton.Visible = false
		frame.CloseButton.Visible = false
		OpenWindow()
		wait(6)
		CloseWindow()
	elseif class == "Passing Time" then
		frame.Header.Text = "Period Over"
		frame.Period.Text = "Next period: "..period.."."
		frame.TeleportButton.Visible = false
		frame.CloseButton.Visible = false
		OpenWindow()
		wait(6)
		CloseWindow()
	elseif class == "School Start" then
		frame.Header.Text = "School Starting"
		frame.Period.Text = "Next period: "..period.."."
		frame.TeleportButton.Visible = false
		frame.CloseButton.Visible = false
		OpenWindow()
		wait(6)
		CloseWindow()
	end
end)

local ptl = game.ReplicatedStorage.PassingTimeLeft
local mpt = ptl.MaxPassingTime

function SetBar()
	bar.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,(mpt.Value - ptl.Value)/mpt.Value * -1)
	if ptl.Value == 0 or frame.Visible then
		bar.Parent:TweenPosition(UDim2.new(0,-15,.6,-140),nil,nil,.4,true)
	else
		bar.Parent:TweenPosition(UDim2.new(0,1,.6,-140),nil,nil,.4,true)
	end
end

ptl.Changed:connect(function()
	SetBar()
end)

local shopname = script.Parent.Name
if shopname:match("ACTIVE_") then
	shopname = string.sub(shopname,8)
end

local shop = game.ReplicatedStorage.Shops:FindFirstChild(shopname)
local gui = script.Parent:WaitForChild("Frame")
local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local enabled = gui:WaitForChild("Enabled")

gui:WaitForChild("Header").Text = shopname

local module = require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.InterfaceModule)
module.loadShopItems(gui,shopname)
module.loadShopTheme(script.Parent,shopname)
module.openWindow(gui)

local char = plr.Character
local torso
if char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
	torso = char.HumanoidRootPart
	gui.ShopPos.Value = char.HumanoidRootPart.Position

function displayFailureWindow(result,reason) --reason == amount of cash needed
	if result == false then
		
		local failure = gui.FailureWindow
		failure.ProductId.Value = 0
		failure.Reason.Text = reason
		failure.NormalButton.Visible = true
		failure.BuyCashButtons.Visible = false
		failure.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until failure.Visible == false
		
	elseif result == "cash" then
		
		local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
		local cash = data.cash.Value
		local products = game.ReplicatedStorage.CashProducts:GetChildren()
		local bestprod
		local highestprod
		for i,v in pairs(products) do
			if v.Name ~= "30" then
				local diff = tonumber(v.Name) - reason
				if diff >= 0 and (bestprod == nil or tonumber(bestprod.Name) > tonumber(v.Name)) then
					bestprod = v
				end
				if highestprod == nil or tonumber(v.Name) > tonumber(highestprod.Name) then
					highestprod = v
				end
			end
		end
		if bestprod == nil then
			bestprod = highestprod
		end
		
		local failure = gui.FailureWindow
		failure.Reason.Text = "You don't have enough Cash to buy this item. Would you like to get more Cash?"
		failure.CashAmount.Value = tonumber(bestprod.Name)
		failure.ProductId.Value = bestprod.Value
		failure.BuyCashButtons.GetCash.Text = "Get +"..bestprod.Name.." Cash!"
		failure.NormalButton.Visible = false
		failure.BuyCashButtons.Visible = true
		failure.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until failure.Visible == false
		
	end
end


gui.BuyRequest.Event:connect(function(button)
	if enabled.Value == true then
		enabled.Value = false
		local oldbg = button.BackgroundColor3
		local oldborder = button.BorderColor3
		button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(.6,.6,.6)
		button.BorderColor3 = Color3.new(.1,.1,.1)
		local result,reason = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PurchaseTemporaryItem:InvokeServer(shop.Name, gui.SelectedItem.Value)
		if result == true then
			local sound = Instance.new("Sound",plr.PlayerGui)
			sound.Name = "ShopBuySuccess"
			sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..game.ReplicatedStorage.AssetIDs[sound.Name].Value
			sound:Play()
			game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound,3)
		else
			displayFailureWindow(result,reason)
		end
		button.BackgroundColor3 = oldbg
		button.BorderColor3 = oldborder
		enabled.Value = true
	end
end)

gui.LoadMenu.Event:connect(function(button) --selected button, get item data
	local data = shop:FindFirstChild(button.Name)
	gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = "Price: $"..data.ItemCost.Value
	gui.Desc.ItemName.Text = button.Name
	gui.Desc.ItemDesc.Text = data.ItemDesc.Value
	gui.Desc.BuyButton.Visible = true
	for i,v in pairs(gui.Items:GetChildren()) do
		v.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
	end
	button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,.25)
	gui.SelectedItem.Value = button.Name
end)

function getItemDataFromID(id)
	for i,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
		if v.ItemID.Value == id then
			return v
		end
	end
end

function clearAllButtons()
	for i,v in pairs(frame:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name ~= "BaseButton" then
			v:Destroy()
		end
	end
	frame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
end

function createButton(obj,hassong)
	local button = frame.BaseButton:clone()
	local num = #frame:GetChildren() - 1
	button.Name = obj.Name
	button.ItemID.Value = obj.ItemID.Value
	button.Position = button.Position + UDim2.new(0,0,0,36*num)
	button.TextLabel.Text = string.sub(obj.Name,7)
	if hassong == true then
		button.TextLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.new(.2,1,.2)
	end
	button.Visible = true
	button.Parent = frame
	frame.CanvasSize = frame.CanvasSize + UDim2.new(0,0,0,36)
end

shopframe.LoadMenu.Event:connect(function(category,search)
	local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	local objdata = game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects
	if data then
		clearAllButtons()
		if category ~= "Search" then
			shopframe.LoadItemInfo:Fire(nil)
		end
		local count = 0
		for _,obj in pairs(objdata:GetChildren()) do
			if obj.Name:match("music_") and ((search ~= nil and string.sub(string.lower(obj.Name),7):match(string.lower(search))) or (category ~= "Search" and obj.Genre.Value == category)) and obj.ItemCost.Value < 1000000 and not obj:FindFirstChild("NotForSale") then
				local hassong = false
				if data.Objects:FindFirstChild(obj.ItemID.Value) then
					hassong = true
				end
				count = count + 1
				createButton(obj,hassong)
			end
		end
		local warning = shopframe:WaitForChild("ItemsBG"):WaitForChild("WarningLabel")
		if count > 0 then
			warning.Visible = false
		else
			if search ~= nil then
				warning.Text = "There are no CDs which match that search. (Can't find it here? Unrestricted DJ Powers let you play any song!)"
			end
			warning.Visible = true
		end
	end
end)


shopframe.LoadItemInfo.Event:connect(function(button)
	if button ~= nil then
		local id = button.ItemID.Value
		shopframe.SelectedID.Value = id
		shopframe.Desc.ItemName.Text = button.TextLabel.Text
		local item = getItemDataFromID(id)
		shopframe.Desc.ItemCost.Text = "Price: $"..item.ItemCost.Value
		shopframe.Desc.BuyButton.Visible = true
		shopframe.Desc.PreviewButton.Visible = true
	else
		shopframe.SelectedID.Value = 0
		shopframe.Desc.ItemName.Text = "Click on a song to get more info"
		shopframe.Desc.ItemCost.Text = ""
		shopframe.Desc.BuyButton.Visible = false
		shopframe.Desc.PreviewButton.Visible = false
	end
end)


shopframe.PreviewMusic.Event:connect(function()
	local item = getItemDataFromID(shopframe.SelectedID.Value)
	if item then
		if script.Parent:FindFirstChild("MusicPreview") then
			script.Parent.MusicPreview:Stop()
			script.Parent.MusicPreview:Destroy()
		end
		if item.AssetID.Value == 142329328 then --WDTFS
			return
		end
		local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
		sound.Name = "MusicPreview"
		sound.Volume = .56 - item.Modifier.Value
		sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..item.AssetID.Value
		sound.Parent = script.Parent
		sound:Play()
		game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound,45)
		wait(40)
		if sound then
			local vol = sound.Volume
			for i=1,20 do
				if sound then
					sound.Volume = sound.Volume - vol/20
					wait(.1)
				end
			end
			if sound then
				sound:Stop()
				sound:Destroy()
			end
		end
	end
end)

			end
		end

		button.BorderColor3 = oldborder

end)

local category = script.Parent:WaitForChild("ItemCategory") --the category of perma-item that should be loaded (pets, gear, etc.)

local shop = {} --table referencing all the objects that belong to the category

for _,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
	if v.Name:match(category.Value.."_") then
		table.insert(shop,v)
	end

local currenttime = os.time()
local sale_end_time = 1482444000
local oldcostname = "BoomboxOldCost" --for showing discounts for certain items in future?


local gui = script.Parent:WaitForChild("Frame")
local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local enabled = gui:WaitForChild("Enabled")

local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:WaitForChild(plr.userId)

gui:WaitForChild("Header").Text = shopname

local details = game.ReplicatedStorage.PermaShopDetails:FindFirstChild(shopname)
if details then
	gui:WaitForChild("SubHeader").Text = details.SubHeader.Value
end

--now load all the buttons
local xv = 0
local yv = 0

for i,v in pairs(shop) do
	if not v:FindFirstChild("NotForSale") and not (v:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and v.ReleaseTime.Value > game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value and game.PlaceId == 9689581) and not (v:FindFirstChild("TimeToGoOffSale") and v.TimeToGoOffSale.Value < game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value) then
		local button = gui.Items.BaseButton:clone() --make new button for each item
		button.Parent = gui.Items
		button.Name = v.Name
		button.ItemID.Value = v.ItemID.Value
		button.Image = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id="..v.DecalID.Value
		button.Position = UDim2.new(0, xv*75, 0, yv*75)
		if v:FindFirstChild("TwitterCode") then
			local bird = script.TwitterBird:clone()
			bird.Parent = button
			bird.Visible = true
		end
		local oldcost = v:FindFirstChild(oldcostname)
		if oldcost and currenttime < sale_end_time and not data.Objects:FindFirstChild(v.ItemID.Value) then
			local percent = math.ceil(-(v.ItemCost.Value - oldcost.Value) / oldcost.Value * 100)
			if percent == 34 then
				percent = 33
			end
			local oc = Instance.new("IntValue")
			oc.Name = "LocalOldCost"
			oc.Value = oldcost.Value
			oc.Parent = button
			button.DiscountPercent.Text = percent.."%"
			button.DiscountBanner.Visible = true
		end
		if v:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*21 and not v:FindFirstChild("TimeToGoOffSale") then
			local newb = script.NewBanner:clone()
			newb.Parent = button
			newb.Visible = true
		elseif v:FindFirstChild("TimeToGoOffSale") then
			local b = script.LimitedBanner:clone()
			b.Parent = button
			b.Visible = true
			local offsaletime = Instance.new("IntValue")
			offsaletime.Name = "OffSaleTime"
			offsaletime.Value = v.TimeToGoOffSale.Value
			offsaletime.Parent = button
		end
		xv = xv + 1
		if xv == 4 then
			xv = 0

		end
		button.Visible = true
	end
end

function setButtonColors()
	for _,button in pairs(gui.Items:GetChildren()) do
		if button.Name ~= "BaseButton" then
			if data.Objects:FindFirstChild(button.ItemID.Value) then --has object
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(.5,1,.5)
			else
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
			end
			if gui.SelectedItem.Value == button.ItemID.Value then
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,.25)
			end
		end
	end
end


function displayFailureWindow(result,reason) --reason == amount of cash needed
	if result == false then
		
		local failure = gui.FailureWindow
		failure.ProductId.Value = 0
		failure.Reason.Text = reason
		failure.NormalButton.Visible = true
		failure.BuyCashButtons.Visible = false
		failure.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until failure.Visible == false
		
	elseif result == "cash" then
		
		local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
		local cash = data.cash.Value
		local products = game.ReplicatedStorage.CashProducts:GetChildren()
		local bestprod
		local highestprod
		for i,v in pairs(products) do
			if v.Name ~= "30" then
				local diff = tonumber(v.Name) - reason
				if diff >= 0 and (bestprod == nil or tonumber(bestprod.Name) > tonumber(v.Name)) then
					bestprod = v
				end
				if highestprod == nil or tonumber(v.Name) > tonumber(highestprod.Name) then
					highestprod = v
				end
			end
		end
		if bestprod == nil then
			bestprod = highestprod
		end
		
		local failure = gui.FailureWindow
		failure.Reason.Text = "You don't have enough Cash to buy this item. Would you like to get more Cash?"
		failure.CashAmount.Value = tonumber(bestprod.Name)
		failure.ProductId.Value = bestprod.Value
		failure.BuyCashButtons.GetCash.Text = "Get +"..bestprod.Name.." Cash!"
		failure.NormalButton.Visible = false
		failure.BuyCashButtons.Visible = true
		failure.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until failure.Visible == false
		
	end
end


gui.BuyRequest.Event:connect(function(button)
	if enabled.Value == true then
		enabled.Value = false
		local oldbg = button.BackgroundColor3
		local oldborder = button.BorderColor3
		button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(.6,.6,.6)
		button.BorderColor3 = Color3.new(.1,.1,.1)
		local result,reason = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PurchasePermanentItem:InvokeServer( gui.SelectedItem.Value) --the item ID
		if result == true then
			local sound = Instance.new("Sound",plr.PlayerGui)
			sound.Name = "ShopBuySuccess"
			sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..game.ReplicatedStorage.AssetIDs[sound.Name].Value
			sound:Play()
			game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound,3)
		else
			displayFailureWindow(result,reason)
		end
		button.BackgroundColor3 = oldbg
		button.BorderColor3 = oldborder
		enabled.Value = true
	end
end)

			gui.Desc.TwitterNotice.Text = ""
		else
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = "Price: $"..itemdata.ItemCost.Value
			gui.Desc.BuyButton.Visible = true
			gui.Desc.TwitterNotice.Text = ""

				gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = "Price: $"..button.LocalOldCost.Value
				gui.Desc.DiscountSlash.Visible = true
				gui.Desc.ItemCostDiscounted.Text = "$"..itemdata.ItemCost.Value

				local timeleft = button.OffSaleTime.Value - game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value
				local days = math.floor(timeleft / 86400)
				local timeleftstr = (days >= 2 and (days.." days")) or (days == 1 and "1 day") or "less than 1 day"
				gui.Desc.LimitedTimeNotice.Text = "Off-sale in: "..timeleftstr
			end
		end
	else
		if data.Objects:FindFirstChild(itemdata.ItemID.Value) then
			gui.Desc.TwitterNotice.Text = "You've unlocked this item from being an RHS Fan Club member!"
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = ""
			gui.Desc.BuyButton.Visible = false
		else
			gui.Desc.TwitterNotice.Text = "Join the RHS Fan Club to unlock this item! (Link in the game description)"
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = ""
			gui.Desc.BuyButton.Visible = false
		end
	end
	
	for i,v in pairs(gui.Items:GetChildren()) do
		v.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
	end
	button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,.25)
	gui.SelectedItem.Value = button.ItemID.Value
	setButtonColors()
end)

local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer

--empty, yellow, purple
local colors = {Color3.new(74/255,58/255,0), Color3.new(1,234/255,0), Color3.new(1,44/255,220/255)}

function commanumber(num)
	local num = tostring(num)
	local i = 0
	local finalstring = ""
	for n in string.gmatch(num,"%d") do
		finalstring = finalstring..n
		i = i + 1
		if (#num - i) % 3 == 0 and i ~= #num then
			finalstring = finalstring..","
		end
	end
	return finalstring
end

function SetBarOverheadText()
	frame:WaitForChild("ConsecDaysCount").Text = stats.Spins.Value == 0 and "Visit 5 days in a row = free RHS prize spins!" or stats.Spins.Value > 0 and "Congrats! You earned "..stats.Spins.Value.." free prize spin"..(stats.Spins.Value == 1 and "" or "s").."!"
end

frame:WaitForChild("LPDayBonus").Text = "(500+"..(stats.ConsecDays.Value > 5 and 5 or stats.ConsecDays.Value) * 100 ..": "..stats.ConsecDays.Value.." Day Bonus)"
local pre_lp = stats.LP.Value - stats.LPReward.Value
frame:WaitForChild("TotalLP").Text = commanumber(pre_lp)
SetBarOverheadText()

	end
end

end

function TextTransparencyFade(element, totalframes, fadein)
	coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
		local i = 0
		local transincrement = (1 - element.TextTransparency) / totalframes
		local strokeincrement = (1 - element.TextStrokeTransparency) / totalframes
		local mult = 1
		if fadein then
			mult = -1
			element.TextTransparency = 1
			element.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
		end
		element.Visible = true
		local render = rs.RenderStepped:connect(function()
			if i < totalframes then
				element.TextTransparency = element.TextTransparency + transincrement * mult
				element.TextStrokeTransparency = element.TextStrokeTransparency + strokeincrement * mult
				i = i + 1
			end
		end)
		repeat wait() until i >= totalframes
		if fadein == false then
			element.Visible = false
			element.TextTransparency = 1 - (transincrement * totalframes)
			element.TextStrokeTransparency = 1 - (strokeincrement * totalframes)
		end
		render:disconnect()
	end),element, totalframes, fadein)
end

function ImageFade(element, totalframes, fadein)
	coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
		local i = 0
		local increment = 1 / totalframes
		local mult = 1
		if fadein then
			mult = -1
			element.ImageTransparency = 1
		else
			element.ImageTransparency = 0
		end
		element.Visible = true
		local render = rs.RenderStepped:connect(function()
			if i < totalframes then
				element.ImageTransparency = element.ImageTransparency + increment * mult
				i = i + 1
			end
		end)
		repeat wait() until i >= totalframes
		if fadein == false then
			element.Visible = false
		end
		render:disconnect()
	end),element, totalframes, fadein)
end

function PlaySound(id,timelength,volume,pitch)
	local sound = Instance.new("Sound",script.Parent)
	sound.Name = "LoyaltySound"
	sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..id
	sound.Volume = volume or 0.5
	sound.Pitch = pitch or 1
	sound:Play()
	game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound,timelength or 3)
end

	{  {"ItemName" = "Generic Item Name", "ItemID" = 210, "ImageID" = "rbxassetid://12345678", "PrizeTier" = 1, "IsCash" = false}, [...]  }

	local prizetable
	
	function ShowSpinPrize(tbl, index, winner)
		local item = tbl[index]
		frame.SpinnerFrame.BackgroundColor3 = spinnercolors[item["PrizeTier"]]
		frame.SpinnerFrame.ItemName.Text = item["ItemName"]
		frame.SpinnerFrame.ItemPic.Image = item["ImageID"]
		PlaySound(203785584,.5,.30,2)
		if winner then --final prize
			PlaySound(131322514,2,.5,1.5)
			if item["PrizeTier"] == 4 then
				PlaySound(345563471,10)
			else
				PlaySound(140413818)
			end
			if item["IsCash"] == true then
				PlaySound(131886985)
			end
			--play star animations?
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PrizeWheelStopped:FireServer()
			SetBarOverheadText()
			frame.SpinnerFrame.Header.Visible = true
			frame.ShopButton.Visible = true
			frame.CloseButton.Visible = true
		else
			frame.SpinnerFrame.Header.Visible = false
		end
	end
	
	frame:TweenSizeAndPosition(UDim2.new(0,360,0,210), UDim2.new(0.5, -180,0.5, -110),...,..., .25,true)
	wait(.25)
	frame.OpenSpinner.Visible = true
	frame.OpenSpinner.MouseButton1Click:connect(function()
		TextTransparencyFade(frame.RHSLPLabel, 20, false)
		TextTransparencyFade(frame.TotalLP, 20, false)
		frame.OpenSpinner.Visible = false
		PlaySound(345593426,nil,.8)
		frame:TweenSizeAndPosition(UDim2.new(0,360,0,240), UDim2.new(0.5, -180,0.5, -125),nil,nil, .4,true)
		wait(.32)
		
		local spinning = false
		local cyclerate = 12
		local winningindex
		
		local function CycleSpinner()
			local recentlyusedtable = {}
			local function DupeCheck(i)
				for _,v in pairs(recentlyusedtable) do
					if v == i then
						return true
					end
				end
				return false
			end
			while spinning do
				local indextodisplay
				local winner = false
				if winningindex ~= nil and DupeCheck(winningindex) == false then
					indextodisplay = winningindex
					winner = true
				else
					repeat
						local n = math.random(1,#prizetable)
						if DupeCheck(n) == false then
							indextodisplay = n
						end
					until indextodisplay ~= nil
				end
				table.insert(recentlyusedtable,indextodisplay)
				if #recentlyusedtable > 5 then
					table.remove(recentlyusedtable,1)
				end
				
				ShowSpinPrize(prizetable,indextodisplay,winner)
				
				if winner then
					spinning = false
					if stats.Spins.Value > 0 then
						frame.RespinButton.Visible = true
					end
					return
				end
				
				wait(1/cyclerate)
			end
		end
		
		local function RunSpin()
			winningindex = nil
			frame.RespinButton.Visible = false
			frame.ShopButton.Visible = false
			frame.CloseButton.Visible = false
			cyclerate = 12
			prizetable = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.FetchSpinPrizeTable:InvokeServer()
			frame.SpinnerFrame.Visible = true
			frame.StopSpinner.Visible = true
			spinning = true
			spawn(CycleSpinner)
			
			local stopcon
			stopcon = frame.StopSpinner.MouseButton1Click:connect(function()
				stopcon:disconnect()
				frame.StopSpinner.Visible = false
				PlaySound(345593426,nil,.8)
				wait(math.random(3,6)/10)
				for i=1,10 do
					wait(.6 + math.random(1,10)/100 - cyclerate / 40)
					cyclerate = cyclerate - 1
				end
				wait(math.random(1,25)/10)
				winningindex = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.FetchWinningSpinPrize:InvokeServer()
				local respincon
				respincon = frame.RespinButton.MouseButton1Click:connect(function()
					if stats.Spins.Value > 0 and spinning == false then
						respincon:disconnect()
						PlaySound(345593426,nil,.8)
						RunSpin()
					end
				end)
			end)
		end
		
		RunSpin()
	end)
end

coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
	while script.Parent ~= nil do
		local timedata = game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData
		local day = data:FindFirstChild("LoyaltyStats") and data.LoyaltyStats.PlayerLoginDay.Value or timedata.CurrentRealLifeDay.Value
		local endtime = timedata.HolidayStartTime.Value + (day * 86400)
		local timeleft = endtime - game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value
		local vals = {math.floor(timeleft/3600), math.floor((timeleft%3600)/60), timeleft%60}
		for i,v in pairs(vals) do
			if #tostring(v) == 1 then
				vals[i] = "0"..v
			end
		end
		if timeleft >= 0 then
			gui.NextLoginTime.Text = "Next daily login reward available in: "..vals[1]..":"..vals[2]..":"..vals[3]
		else
			gui.NextLoginTime.Text = "Daily login reward available! Rejoin the game to claim it."
		end
		wait(1)
	end
end))

local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local shopframe = script.Parent:WaitForChild("Frame")
local frame = shopframe:WaitForChild("CarSelectHolder"):WaitForChild("ScrollingFrame")
local enabled = shopframe:WaitForChild("Enabled")
local selectedcar = plr:WaitForChild("SelectedCar")

local backbutton = shopframe:WaitForChild("BackButton")
local forwardbutton = shopframe:WaitForChild("ForwardButton")

local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:WaitForChild(plr.userId)

local module = require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.InterfaceModule)
module.openWindow(script.Parent.Frame)

function getItemDataFromID(id)
	for i,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
		if v.ItemID.Value == id then
			return v
		end
	end
end


function displayFailureWindow(result,reason) --reason == amount of cash needed
	if result == false then
		
		local failure = shopframe.FailureWindow
		failure.ProductId.Value = 0
		failure.Reason.Text = reason
		failure.NormalButton.Visible = true
		failure.BuyCashButtons.Visible = false
		failure.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until failure.Visible == false
		
	elseif result == "cash" then
		
		local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
		local cash = data.cash.Value
		local products = game.ReplicatedStorage.CashProducts:GetChildren()
		local bestprod
		local highestprod
		for i,v in pairs(products) do
			local diff = tonumber(v.Name) - reason
			if diff >= 0 and (bestprod == nil or tonumber(bestprod.Name) > tonumber(v.Name)) then
				bestprod = v
			end
			if highestprod == nil or tonumber(v.Name) > tonumber(highestprod.Name) then
				highestprod = v
			end
		end
		if bestprod == nil then
			bestprod = highestprod
		end
		
		local failure = shopframe.FailureWindow
		failure.Reason.Text = "You don't have enough Cash to buy this item. Would you like to get more Cash?"
		failure.CashAmount.Value = tonumber(bestprod.Name)
		failure.ProductId.Value = bestprod.Value
		failure.BuyCashButtons.GetCash.Text = "Get +"..bestprod.Name.." Cash!"
		failure.NormalButton.Visible = false
		failure.BuyCashButtons.Visible = true
		failure.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until failure.Visible == false
		
	end
end


function setButtonColors()
	for _,button in pairs(frame:GetChildren()) do
		if button.Name ~= "BaseButton" then
			if data.Objects:FindFirstChild(button.ItemID.Value) then --has object
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(.5,1,.5)
				button.ItemCost.Text = ""
			else
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
			end
			if button.ItemID.Value == 288 then
				button.ItemCost.Text = ""
			end
			if plr.SelectedCar.Value == button.ItemID.Value then
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,.25)
			end
		end
	end
end

function setNavigationButtonColors()
	local canvaswidth = frame.CanvasSize.X.Offset
	local canvaspos = frame.CanvasPosition.X
	local framewidth = frame.AbsoluteSize.X
	
	local isat
	
	if canvaspos == 0 then
		isat = 0
	elseif canvaspos >= canvaswidth - framewidth then
		isat = 1
	end
	
	if isat == 0 then
		backbutton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(107, 120, 245)
		backbutton.ImageTransparency = .7
	else
		backbutton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(128, 244, 120)
		backbutton.ImageTransparency = 0
	end
	
	if isat == 1 then
		forwardbutton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(107, 120, 245)
		forwardbutton.ImageTransparency = .7
	else
		forwardbutton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(128, 244, 120)
		forwardbutton.ImageTransparency = 0
	end
end

shopframe.LoadMenu.Event:connect(function() --create all buttons
	--first, clear any existing buttons
	shopframe:WaitForChild("BuyButton").Visible = false
	shopframe:WaitForChild("ItemDesc").Text = ""
	for i,v in pairs(frame:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name ~= "BaseButton" then
			v:Destroy()
		end
	end
	frame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
	
	local objectstable = {}
	
	local objdata = game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects
	for _,v in pairs(objdata:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name:match("vehic_") and not (v:FindFirstChild("OldSchoolCar")) and not (v:FindFirstChild("NotForSale") and data.Objects:FindFirstChild(v.ItemID.Value) == nil) and not ((v:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v.ReleaseTime.Value < 0) and game.PlaceId == 9689581) and not (v:FindFirstChild("TimeToGoOffSale") and v.TimeToGoOffSale.Value < game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value and data.Objects:FindFirstChild(v.ItemID.Value) == nil) then
			table.insert(objectstable,v)
		end
	end
	
	local function sortfunc(v1, v2)
		if (v1:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v1.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*24) or (v2:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v2.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*24) then
			if v1:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v1.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*24 and (v2:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") == nil or game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v2.ReleaseTime.Value > 86400*24) then
				return true
			elseif v2:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v2.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*24 and (v1:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") == nil or game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v1.ReleaseTime.Value > 86400*24) then
				return false
			elseif v1:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v1.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*24 and v2:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v2.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*24 then
				if v1.ReleaseTime.Value > v2.ReleaseTime.Value then
					return true
				elseif v1.ReleaseTime.Value < v2.ReleaseTime.Value then
					return false
				else
					if v1.ItemID.Value > v2.ItemID.Value then
						return true
					else
						return false
					end
				end
			end
		end
		
		if v1.ItemCost.Value < v2.ItemCost.Value then
			return true
		else
			return false
		end
	end
	
	table.sort(objectstable,sortfunc)
	
	for _,v in pairs(objectstable) do
		local button = frame.BaseButton:clone()
		button.Image = "rbxassetid://"..v.DecalID.Value
		button.ItemID.Value = v.ItemID.Value
		if not data.Objects:FindFirstChild(v.ItemID.Value) then --show cost
			button.ItemCost.Text = "$"..v.ItemCost.Value
		end
		button.Position = UDim2.new(0,(#frame:GetChildren() - 1) * 100,0,0)
		button.Name = string.sub(v.Name,7)
		button.Parent = frame
		if (v:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*24) or v:FindFirstChild("TimeToGoOffSale") then
			if v:FindFirstChild("TimeToGoOffSale") then
				button.LimitedBanner.Visible = true
				local offsaletime = Instance.new("IntValue")
				offsaletime.Name = "OffSaleTime"
				offsaletime.Value = v.TimeToGoOffSale.Value
				offsaletime.Parent = button
			else
				button.NewBanner.Visible = true
			end
			
			for _,x in pairs(frame:GetChildren()) do
				if x.Name ~= "BaseButton" then
					if x ~= button then
						x.Position = x.Position + UDim2.new(0,100,0,0)
					end
				end
			end
			button.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
		end
		if v:FindFirstChild("TwitterCode") then
			local icon = script.TwitterBird:clone()
			icon.Parent = button
			icon.Visible = true
			for _,x in pairs(frame:GetChildren()) do
				if x.Name ~= "BaseButton" then
					if x ~= button then
						x.Position = x.Position + UDim2.new(0,100,0,0)
					end
				end
			end
			button.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
		end
			
		button.Visible = true
	end
	frame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0,(#frame:GetChildren() - 1) * 100,0,0)
	
	local id = selectedcar.Value
	local item = getItemDataFromID(id)
	if item then
		if data.Objects:FindFirstChild(id) then
			shopframe.BuyButton.Text = "Spawn"
		else
			if not item:FindFirstChild("TwitterCode") then
				shopframe.BuyButton.Text = "Buy Car"
			else
				shopframe.BuyButton.Visible = false
			end
		end
		if item:FindFirstChild("OffSaleTime") then
			local timeleft = item.OffSaleTime.Value - game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value
			local days = math.floor(timeleft / 86400)
			local timeleftstr = (days >= 2 and (days.." days")) or (days == 1 and "1 day") or "less than 1 day"
			shopframe.LimitedTimeNotice.Text = "Off-sale in: "..timeleftstr
		end
		shopframe.BuyButton.Visible = true
		shopframe.ItemDesc.Text = item.ItemDesc.Value
		shopframe.Header.Text = string.sub(item.Name,7)
	end
	setButtonColors()
	setNavigationButtonColors()
end)

shopframe.MainButtonPress.Event:connect(function(button)
	if enabled.Value == true then
		enabled.Value = false
		local oldbg = button.BackgroundColor3
		local oldborder = button.BorderColor3
		button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(.6,.6,.6)
		button.BorderColor3 = Color3.new(.1,.1,.1)
		
		if data.Objects:FindFirstChild(selectedcar.Value) then --has car, get the spawn tool
			local bp = plr:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
			local char = plr.Character
			
			if bp and char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and not char:FindFirstChild("Spawn Car") then
				if bp:FindFirstChild("Spawn Car") then
					char.Humanoid:EquipTool(bp["Spawn Car"])
				else
					local tool = script.Parent["Spawn Car"]:clone()
					tool.Parent = bp
					wait()
					char.Humanoid:EquipTool(tool)
				end
			end
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.ChangeSelectedCar:FireServer(selectedcar.Value)
			module.closeWindow(script.Parent)
			return
		else
			local result,reason = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PurchasePermanentItem:InvokeServer(selectedcar.Value)
			if result == true then
				local sound = Instance.new("Sound",plr.PlayerGui)
				sound.Name = "ShopBuySuccess"
				sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..game.ReplicatedStorage.AssetIDs[sound.Name].Value --better change this in all scripts!
				sound:Play()
				game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound,3)
				shopframe.BuyButton.Text = "Spawn"
			else
				displayFailureWindow(result,reason)
			end
		end
		
		
		button.BackgroundColor3 = oldbg
		button.BorderColor3 = oldborder
		enabled.Value = true
	end
end)

function scrollFrameByButton(val)
	frame.CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(frame.CanvasPosition.X + (100 * val),0)
end

	scrollFrameByButton(1)
end)

backbutton.MouseButton1Click:connect(function()
	scrollFrameByButton(-1)
end)

	if property == "CanvasPosition" then
		setNavigationButtonColors()
	end
end)w

local songs = {} --the sets in the order that they should play based on the rng

local rng = game.ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("RNG").Value
local weekdayspassed = game.ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("WeekdaysPassed")
local musicsets = { --4 tables of each music set (1-3 weekdays, 4 weekends) (overnight to evening)
	{503064767,503065215,503066771,503063705},
	{503064767,503064607,503066799,503063839},
	{503064767,503065423,503066871,503063867},
	{503065984,503065630,503066928,503064485},

	neg = -1
end

	songs[i] = musicsets[(rng + i * neg) % 3 + 1]
end

local fading = false
local location
--Outside, Club Red, Madi's Music

local avg_loudness = 0
local highest_loudness = 0

local shifting = false --whether the volume is currently being shifted

local defaultid = "rbxassetid://143080540" --the default club music

local current_club_song
local current_queue_item

local current_boombox_sound
local boombox_player

local loudnesses = {}


function fadeSong(sound)
	fading = true
	shifting = true
	local basevol = sound.Volume
	for i=1,20 do
		if sound then
			sound.Volume = sound.Volume - basevol / 20
			wait(.14)
		end
	end
	if sound then
		sound:Destroy()
	end
	shifting = false
	fading = false
end

local firsttime
if script.Parent:FindFirstChild("ACTIVE_IntroGUI") then
	firsttime = true
end

--Shift the volume of the sound according to what the current highest loudness is
--(If starting is true, volume can be shifted up)

function ShiftVolume(sound, starting)
	if sound == nil or shifting == true or sound.SoundId == defaultid then return end
	shifting = true
	local target_volume
	
	if highest_loudness <= 200 then
		target_volume = .9
	elseif highest_loudness <= 300 then
		target_volume = .8 + (300 - highest_loudness) / 1000
	elseif highest_loudness <= 350 then
		target_volume = .7 + (350 - highest_loudness) / 500
	elseif highest_loudness <= 400 then
		target_volume = .6 + (400 - highest_loudness) / 500
	elseif highest_loudness <= 450 then
		target_volume = .5 + (450 - highest_loudness) / 500
	elseif highest_loudness <= 500 then
		target_volume = .5
	elseif highest_loudness <= 675 then
		target_volume = .15 + (675 - highest_loudness) / 500
	elseif highest_loudness > 675 then
		target_volume = .1
	end
	
	if starting ~= true and sound.Volume <= target_volume then shifting = false return end
	
	local vol = sound.Volume
	for i=1,7 do
		if sound then
			sound.Volume = sound.Volume - ((vol - target_volume) / 7)
			wait(1/32)
		end
	end
	
	if target_volume == .1 then --too loud
		if sound and sound.Name == "ClubMusic" then
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.FlagSong:FireServer()
		elseif sound and sound.Name == "BGM" then
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.FlagBoomboxSong:FireServer(boombox_player)
		end
	end
	
	shifting = false
end

function createBackgroundMusic(id,isboombox,musicset,segment)
	if not script.Parent:FindFirstChild("BGM") then
		if script.Parent:FindFirstChild("ClubMusic") and not fading then
			fadeSong(script.Parent.ClubMusic)
		end
		current_club_song = nil
		if firsttime and not isboombox then
			--preload the BGM upon first joining game
			local s = Instance.new("Sound",script.Parent)
			s.SoundId = id
			s.Volume = .001
			s:Play()
			game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(s,.1)
			wait(4)
			firsttime = false
		end
		local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
		sound.SoundId = id
		sound.Volume = (isboombox and 0) or 10 / 7
		sound.Looped = true
		sound.SoundGroup = game.Soundscape.BackgroundMusic
		sound.Name = "BGM"
		sound.Parent = script.Parent
		local i = 0
		while (sound and sound.IsLoaded == false) or i == 90 do
			wait(1/30)
			i = i + 1
		end
		if isboombox and isboombox.TimePosition > 3.5 then
			sound.TimePosition = isboombox.TimePosition
		end
		if isboombox then
			current_boombox_sound = sound
			highest_loudness = 0
			avg_loudness = 0
			loudnesses = {}
		else
			current_boombox_sound = nil
		end
		sound:Play()
		if not isboombox then
			--preload the next song
			local nextsongid
			local t = game.Lighting:GetMinutesAfterMidnight()
			if day.Value >= 6 and t >= 1050 and t < 1380 then
				musicset = 4
				segment = 1
			else
				segment = segment + 1
				if segment > 4 then
					segment = 1
					musicset = musicset + 1
					if musicset > 4 then
						musicset = 1
					end
				end
			end
			nextsongid = songs[musicset][segment]
			if nextsongid then
				local sound = Instance.new("Sound",script.Parent)
				sound.Volume = .001
				sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..nextsongid
				sound:Play()
				game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound,.1)
			end
		end
		if isboombox and vol.Value == 0 then
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.LocalNotification:Fire("Your Background Music volume is set to 0%, so you will not hear the Boombox.")
		end
		if isboombox then
			shifting = true
			for i = 1,10 do
				if sound then
					sound.Volume = sound.Volume + 0.06 -- go to 0.6 volume
					wait(1/32)
				end
			end
			shifting = false
			
			ShiftVolume(sound, true)
		end
	end
end


-- Create Club Red DJ music, getting the ID from the queue 

function createClubMusic() 
	if not script.Parent:FindFirstChild("ClubMusic") then
		current_boombox_sound = nil
		loudnesses = {}
		avg_loudness = 0
		highest_loudness = 0
		
		local queueitem
		local nextsong
		preloaded_next_song = false
		for _,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.SongQueueItems:GetChildren()) do
			if v.QueuePosition.Value == 1 then
				queueitem = v
			elseif v.QueuePosition.Value == 2 then
				nextsong = v 
			end
		end
		
		local starttime
		if queueitem then
			current_queue_item = queueitem
			starttime = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.GetTimeOfSong:InvokeServer()
			if starttime < 1.5 then starttime = 0 end
		end
		
		local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
		sound.Name = "ClubMusic"
		if queueitem then
			sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..queueitem.SongID.Value
		else
			sound.SoundId = defaultid
		end
		sound.Volume = 0
		sound.TimePosition = starttime
		sound.SoundGroup = game.Soundscape.ClubMusic
		sound.Looped = true
		sound.Parent = script.Parent
		current_club_song = sound
		while (sound and sound.IsLoaded == false) do
			wait(1/30)
		end
		if not sound then return end
		sound:Play()
		
		if nextsong then
			-- Preload the next song 
			local temp = Instance.new("Sound", script.Parent)
			temp.Volume = 0.001
			temp.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..nextsong.SongID.Value
			temp:Play()
			game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(temp, .1)
			preloaded_next_song = true
		end
		
		shifting = true
		for i = 1,10 do
			if sound then
				sound.Volume = sound.Volume + 0.06 -- go to 0.6 volume
				wait(1/32)
			end
		end
		shifting = false
		
		ShiftVolume(sound, true)
	end
end



function getBackgroundMusic() --get BG music based on time/day
	local day = game.ReplicatedStorage.Day.Value
	local wp = weekdayspassed.Value
	
	local t = game.Lighting:GetMinutesAfterMidnight()
	if t >= 1380 then --get next day's music (late night)
		day = day + 1
		if day == 8 then
			day = 1
		end
	end
	
	local musicset
	if day == 1 or day == 7 then
		musicset = 4
	else
		musicset = (weekdayspassed.Value - 1) % 3 + 1
	end
	
	local segment
	if t >= 1380 or t < 300 then
		segment = 1
	elseif t >= 300 and t < 660 then
		segment = 2
	elseif t >= 660 and t < 1050 then
		segment = 3
	elseif t >= 1050 and t < 1380 then
		segment = 4
	end
	
	local id = "rbxassetid://"..songs[musicset][segment]
	return id,musicset,segment
end

function isInClub(pos)
	if require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.PositionIsInsideArea)(pos,"Club Red") then
		return true
	else
		return false
	end
end

function isInSchool(pos) --and during class hours
	local day = game.ReplicatedStorage.Day.Value
	local t = game.Lighting:GetMinutesAfterMidnight()
	if require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.PositionIsInsideArea)(pos,"School") and day > 1 and day < 7 and t >= 420 and t < 900 then
		return true
	else
		return false
	end
end


game.Soundscape.ClubMusic.Volume = clubvol.Value / 10
game.Soundscape.BackgroundMusic.Volume = vol.Value / 10

vol.Changed:connect(function(val)
	game.Soundscape.BackgroundMusic.Volume = vol.Value / 10
end)

clubvol.Changed:connect(function(val)
	game.Soundscape.ClubMusic.Volume = clubvol.Value / 10
end)

-- Constantly check for club sound volume

coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
	while true do
		wait(1/12)
		local song = current_club_song or current_boombox_sound
		if (song and song.PlaybackLoudness >= -1) then
			table.insert(loudnesses, song.PlaybackLoudness)
			if #loudnesses > 10 then
				table.remove(loudnesses, 1)
			end
			local avg = 0
			for _,v in pairs(loudnesses) do
				avg = avg + v 
			end
			avg = avg / #loudnesses
			avg_loudness = avg 
			--print("avg_loudness: ".. avg_loudness .." - highest_loudness: ".. highest_loudness .. "num loudnesses: ".. #loudnesses)
			if #loudnesses >= 8 and highest_loudness < avg then
				--print("highest loudness exceeded, shifting")
				highest_loudness = avg 
				ShiftVolume(song)
			end
		end
	end
end))



while true do --main function at the bottom, constantly checking
	wait(.25)
	local char = plr.Character
	if char and char.Parent ~= nil and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
		local torso = char.HumanoidRootPart
		local newlocation = "Outside"
		if isInClub(torso.Position) then
			newlocation = "Club Red"
		end
		
		if newlocation and (newlocation ~= location or newlocation == "Outside") then
			location = newlocation
			if location == "Outside" then
				
				local newsong,musicset,segment = getBackgroundMusic() --returns soundid
				local isboombox
				
				if boomboxsetting.Value == true and not isInSchool(torso.Position) then
					for _,v in pairs(game.Players:GetChildren()) do
						if v.Character and v.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
							local boombox = v.Character:FindFirstChild("Boombox") or v.Character:FindFirstChild("Golden Boombox")
							if boombox and (torso.Position - v.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).magnitude < 40 then
								local boomsound = boombox:FindFirstChild("BoomboxSound")
								if boomsound then
									local id = boomsound.SoundId
									if id ~= "" then
										isboombox = boomsound
										newsong = id
										break
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
				
				local bgm = script.Parent:FindFirstChild("BGM")
				if bgm and bgm.SoundId == newsong then
					--no change
				elseif bgm and bgm.SoundId ~= newsong then
					fadeSong(bgm)
					createBackgroundMusic(newsong,isboombox,musicset,segment)
					if isboombox then
						boombox_player = game.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(isboombox.Parent.Parent)
					end
				else
					createBackgroundMusic(newsong,isboombox,musicset,segment)
				end
			
			elseif location == "Club Red" then
				
				local bgm = script.Parent:FindFirstChild("BGM")
				if bgm then
					fadeSong(bgm)
				end
				createClubMusic()
				
			end
			
		elseif newlocation == "Club Red" then
			-- do checks on the current song playing - whether it's time to switch, or preload new song 
			local queueitem
			local nextsong
			for _,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.SongQueueItems:GetChildren()) do
				if v.QueuePosition.Value == 1 then
					queueitem = v
				elseif v.QueuePosition.Value == 2 then
					nextsong = v 
				end
			end
			
			if queueitem ~= current_queue_item then --song change
				current_queue_item = queueitem
				if script.Parent:FindFirstChild("ClubMusic") then
					fadeSong(script.Parent.ClubMusic)
				end
				createClubMusic()
			end
			
			if nextsong and not preloaded_next_song then --preload 
				local sound = Instance.new("Sound",script.Parent)
				sound.Volume = .001
				sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..nextsong.SongID.Value
				sound:Play()
				game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound,.1)
				preloaded_next_song = true
			end
		end
		
	end
end

local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local mouse = plr:GetMouse()
local enabled = true
local playermouse

local range = script.Parent:WaitForChild("TeleportRange").Value

local cd = 2

local localeffect = plr.PlayerGui:WaitForChild("TeleportEffect")

function standUp(char)
	if char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
		char.Humanoid.Jump = true
	end
end

function checkIfStanding(char)
	if char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
		if char.Humanoid.SeatPart == nil then
			return true
		else
			return false
		end
	end
end

function enable()
	if playermouse then
		playermouse.Icon = "rbxasset://textures\\GunCursor.png"
	end
	enabled = true
end

function disable()
	if playermouse then
		playermouse.Icon = "rbxasset://textures\\GunWaitCursor.png"
	end
	enabled = false
end

function activate(targetpos,mag)
	local char = plr.Character
	if char and enabled then
		local torso = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local hum = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
		if torso and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
			disable()
			
			local origin = torso.Position
			local ray1 = Ray.new(origin, (targetpos - origin).unit * mag)
			
			local ignorelist = {}
	
			for i,v in pairs(game.Players:GetChildren()) do
				if v.Character then
					table.insert(ignorelist,v.Character)
				end
			end
			
			local hit,destination
			local collision = false
			
			hit,destination = workspace:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(ray1,ignorelist)
			if hit then
				destination = destination - ray1.Unit.Direction * 1.5
				collision = true
			end
			
			if collision then
				local neworigin = targetpos + Vector3.new(0,17,0)
				local ray2 = Ray.new(neworigin, ((neworigin - Vector3.new(0,17,0)) - neworigin).unit * 16)
				local hit2,destination2
				hit2,destination2 = workspace:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(ray2, ignorelist)
				if not hit2 then
					destination = targetpos
				end
			end
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PlayerHasTeleported:FireServer(origin,destination,script.Parent)
			
			localeffect:Fire(origin,destination,script.Parent)
			if checkIfStanding(char) == false then
				standUp(char)
			end
			wait(.15)
			if torso and checkIfStanding(char) == true then
				torso.CFrame = CFrame.new(destination)
			end
			
			wait(cd)
			
			enable()
			
		end
	end
end


script.Parent.Equipped:connect(function(mouse)
	playermouse = mouse
	equipped = true
	mouse.Button1Down:connect(function()
		if enabled and equipped then
			local char = plr.Character
			if char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
				
				local mag = (char.HumanoidRootPart.Position - (mouse.Hit.p + Vector3.new(0,3,0))).magnitude
				if mag > range then
					mag = range
				end
				local ray = Ray.new(char.HumanoidRootPart.Position, (mouse.Hit.p + Vector3.new(0,3,0) - char.HumanoidRootPart.Position).unit * mag)
				local target = char.HumanoidRootPart.Position + (mouse.Hit.p + Vector3.new(0,3,0) - char.HumanoidRootPart.Position).unit * mag

				activate(target,mag)
			end
			
		end
	end)
end)

script.Parent.Unequipped:connect(function()
	playermouse = nil
	equipped = false
end)

local selectionBox
local selectionLasso

local PBGui

local selectedButtonTable = {}
local oldButton = nil
function onMouseLeave(hoverSelection)
	if oldButton ~= nil then
		local notSelected = true
		local selectionText = ""
		for key, value in pairs(selectedButtonTable) do
			if oldButton == value then
				notSelected = false
			else
				selectionText = value.BackgroundColor.Name
			end
		end
		if notSelected then
			hoverSelection.Text = selectionText
			oldButton.Parent.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.Black()
		end
	end
	oldButton = nil
end

function onMouseEnter(hoverSelection, guiButton)
	onMouseLeave(hoverSelection)
	hoverSelection.Text = guiButton.BackgroundColor.Name
	if guiButton ~= selectedButton then
		guiButton.Parent.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.White()
		oldButton = guiButton
	end
end

function onMouseUp(colorHolder, paletteFrame, guiButton)
	if selectedButtonTable[colorHolder] ~= nil then
		selectedButtonTable[colorHolder].Parent.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.Black()
	end
	guiButton.Parent.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.Yellow()
	colorHolder.BackgroundColor = guiButton.BackgroundColor
	selectionBox.Color = guiButton.BackgroundColor
	selectionLasso.Color = guiButton.BackgroundColor
	Tool.Value.Value = guiButton.BackgroundColor
	selectedButtonTable[colorHolder] = guiButton
	
	onMouseLeavePalette(paletteFrame)
	
	print("Selected Color: " .. guiButton.BackgroundColor.Name)
end

function onShowColorDialog(paletteFrame)
	paletteFrame.Visible = true
end

	unsetSelectionBox()
	selectionBox.Adornee = part.Parent
	selectionLasso.Part = part.Parent.PrimaryPart
end
function unsetSelectionBox() 
	selectionBox.Adornee = nil
	selectionLasso.Part = nil
end

			if sideBar and primaryColor and sideBar.Parent ~= nil and paletteFrame and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1 and not gameprocessed then
				if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonX then
					if paletteFrame.Visible then
						paletteFrame.Visible = false
					else
						paletteFrame.Visible = true
						local toselect = primaryColor
						for _,v in pairs(paletteFrame:GetChildren()) do
							if v:IsA("Frame") and v:FindFirstChild("TextButton") then
								if v.TextButton.BackgroundColor3 == primaryColor.BackgroundColor then
									toselect = v
								end
							end
						end
						GuiService.SelectedObject = toselect
					end
				elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonB or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonSelect then
					if paletteFrame.Visible then
						paletteFrame.Visible = false
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
	
	mainFrame.MouseLeave:connect(function() onMouseLeave(hoverSelection) end)

end

	end

	end
end

function on3dButton1Down(mouse) 
	mouseDown = true
	while mouseDown do
		local canvas = mouse.Target
		local pos = mouse.Hit.p
		if canvas and canvas.Name == "!Canvas" then
			if lastposition == nil or (lastposition - pos).magnitude > .033 then
				Paint(canvas,pos)
			end
		else
			mouseDown = false
		end
		wait(1/30)
	end
	
end

	mouseDown = false
	lastposition = nil
end

local guiMain
function onEquippedLocal(mouse)
	
	local character = script.Parent.Parent
	local player = game.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(character)
	
	PBGui = script.Parent:WaitForChild("PaintbrushGui"):clone()
	PBGui.Parent = player.PlayerGui
	PBGui.AttachedTool.Value = script.Parent

	guiMain = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
	guiMain.Name = "PaintBucketGui"
	guiMain.Parent = player.PlayerGui

	inGui = false
	inPalette = false
	mouseDown = false
	
	local uis = game:GetService("UserInputService")
	if (not uis.TouchEnabled) and (uis.KeyboardEnabled or uis.GamepadEnabled) then
		mouse.Button1Down:connect(function() on3dButton1Down(mouse) end)
		mouse.Button1Up:connect(function() on3dButton1Up(mouse) end)
	end
	
	mouse.Move:connect(function() on3dMouseMove(mouse) end)
	mouse.Icon = "rbxasset://textures\\GunCursor.png"
	mouse.TargetFilter = workspace.ArtCanvases

	selectionBox = Instance.new("SelectionBox")
	selectionBox.Color = Tool.Value.Value
	selectionBox.Adornee = nil
	selectionBox.Parent = player.PlayerGui

	selectionLasso = Instance.new("SelectionPartLasso")
	selectionLasso.Name = "Model Delete Lasso"
	selectionLasso.Humanoid = character.Humanoid
	selectionLasso.Part = nil
	selectionLasso.Visible = true
	selectionLasso.archivable = false
	selectionLasso.Color = Tool.Value.Value
	selectionLasso.Parent = game.workspace
	
	buildGui(guiMain, mouse)
end

function onUnequippedLocal()
	guiMain:Remove()
	selectionBox:Remove()
	selectionLasso:Remove()
	if PBGui then
		PBGui:Destroy()
	end
end

if uis.TouchEnabled and not (uis.KeyboardEnabled or uis.GamepadEnabled) then
	local emulation = plr:FindFirstChild("KeyboardEmulation")
	if emulation then
		local mouseisdown = emulation:WaitForChild("MouseIsDown")
		local mouse = plr:GetMouse()

			wait(1/31)

				local canvas = mouse.Target
				local pos = mouse.Hit.p
				if canvas and canvas.Name == "!Canvas" then
					if lastposition == nil or (lastposition - pos).magnitude > .033 then

						cameralock.Name = "CameraLock"
						game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(cameralock,1)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end
